Let's compare LP oracle with previous. This is a Frank-Wolfe step for minimizing maximum of active linearized convolutions. It may be more direct and improve local. We should implement and test mentally.

Pseudo:
def lp_active_direction(y, c, M, K, time_limit, solver_pref):
    n=len(y)
    peaks=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
    rhs_base=2*M
    A rows.
    c_obj = np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1
    bounds = [(0,1)]*n + [(None,None)]
    A_ub rows: row coeffs y_slice in cols i; -1 in tau col; ub=rhs_base
    A_eq = [[1...1,0]]
    res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.full(K,rhs_base), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9,...})
    if success: g=res.x[:n]; tau=res.x[-1]
    else None
Return g,tau,peaks

Line search implementation:
```
def line_search_concave(y,g,M):
    # evaluate M at alpha candidates
    alphas = [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.5,0.7,0.3]
    best_a=None; best_M=M+1
    for a in alphas:
       trial = (1-a)*y + a*g
       # ensure nonneg sum
       if trial.min() < -1e-12: continue
       trial = np.maximum(trial,0)
       s=trial.sum(); if s<1e-15 continue
       trial /= s
       # optional if a<1, maybe not exact convex combo sum; normalize
       b=fast_conv(trial); m=b.max()
       if m < best_M: ...
    return best_a,best_M
```
Since (1-a) and a nonneg, no clip until normalization (sum remains 1 exactly if sums 1, but due float). Could allow a >1 if g-y has min ratio? We can compute max_alpha such that trial>=0: For nonzero g_y? If g_i=0 and y_i>0, ratio? trial_i = y_i + a(g_i-y_i). If g_i-y_i negative, a ≤ y_i/(y_i-g_i). We can compute max extrapolation up to e.g. 2. But convex a≤1 safe.

Entropy smoothed descent:
```
def entropy_smooth(y, time_limit, beta_start=5,beta_end=300,growth=1.02, step_base=None, sweep=...):
   y=norm_sum1(y).astype
   M=fast_max(y)
   best=y
   logy=np.log(np.maximum(y,1e-30))
   eta=0.5
   for iter:
      b=fast_conv(y); bm=b.max()
      p=np.exp(beta*(b-bm)); p/=p.sum()
      # sym p? b symmetric, p symmetric. p full length L.
      # grad = 2*corr(y,p)
      grad = compute_grad(y,p)
      # maybe normalize grad to Lipschitz: eta=0.1/(1+np.max(np.abs(grad)))? 
      # update logy = logy - eta*grad
      # to prevent under/overflow: logy -= max, clip [logtiny, log1]
      new = np.exp(clip(logy, -700, 0)); s=sum; if tiny fallback
      # Re-normalize, maybe maintain positive floor? If many zeros, add tiny to best? 
      # line search over eta? Choose from [eta*2,eta,eta/2...], pick best M.
      if Mnew < M: accept, update logy
   return best
```

FISTA exact gradient of soft max:
```
def fista_smooth(y0, time_limit, beta_start=10,beta_end=500,growth=1.01, max_iter, use_full=True, top_k=None):
   n=len(y); x=norm_sum1(y0); x_prev=x; t=1; best=x; bestM=...
   beta=beta_start
   last_bb=None
   eta=1.0
   for it:
      y = x + (t_prev/t_new)*(x-x_prev); y=max(y,0); normalize
      b=fast_conv(y); bm=b.max(); p=softmax(beta*(b-bm))
      grad=2*cross(y,p)  # length n
      # Optional: if p full, grad sum? 
      # Step size: backtracking on M
      candidates = [eta, eta*2, eta*0.5, eta*0.75, eta*1.25] maybe plus based on norm
      # For each eta_c: z=y - eta_c*grad + tiny; x_new=proj_simplex(z); m=fast_max; select best; if best < current, update eta adapt up/down.
      # FISTA update t_new=...
   restarts with noise.
```

```
def active_lp_descent(a, time_limit, smart=True):
   a = norm_sum1(a)
   b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max(); best=a; best_M=M
   no_improve=0
   K=128
   it=0
   t0=time.monotonic()
   while time < time_limit and no_improve < max_iter?
       b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max()
       if M < best_M: best...
       peaks=select_peaks(b,M,K)
       g, tau = get_lp_direction(a,b,M,peaks, timeout)
       if g is None:
           # fallback gradient direction
           g = gradient_direction(a,b,peaks)
       if g is None: break
       # line search
       trialM = M
       for alpha in ...:
          z=(1-alpha)*a+alpha*g; z=clip; normalize; m=fast_max(z)
          if m < trialM: accept
       if accepted:
          a=best_trial; M=trialM; no_improve=0
          if M<best_M: best...
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve==chain: K*2 or fallback
       it+=1
       # print every 20
   return best
```

def projected_top_descent(a, time_limit, K=128, beta=20):
   a=norm_sum1; best=a; best_M=fast_max(a); b=fast_conv(a); no_impr=0; eta=1.0
   while time:
      b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max(); if M<best...
      peaks=top K
      # weights softmax over b[peaks] with beta? beta can anneal from 5 to 200.
      vv=b[peaks]-b[peaks].max(); p=exp(eta_w*vv); p/=sum
      grad=grad_top(a,peaks,p)  # 2 included? if p weights for objective max? If p approximate subgradient, use grad=2*...
      # choose step candidates from norm: gnorm = max(abs(grad)); candidates = [1e-3,1e-2,0.1,1,10,100]*1/gnorm? But projection handles.
      # Actually step dimension: grad magnitude ~M; to move by O(1/n) ~1e-4, step ~1. If gradmax 2e-4, step 1 => move 2e-4.
      tried base = 1.0 / (gradmax+1e-15)  # if 2e-4 ->5000? Actually 1/gradmax=5000; maybe too large. We want move fraction? The simplex projection of a - step*grad. Projection_simplex: use standard sort; but called many. Optimize with `np.sort` each. We can also use `np.partition`? Standard:
```
def project_simplex(v, pos=True):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    v_sorted=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(v_sorted)
    rho=np.nonzero(v_sorted*np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv-1))[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return ones/n
    r=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[r]-1)/(r+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    s=w.sum(); if s<=0: return ones/n
    return w/s  # numerical
```
This projects onto sum=1 nonneg. It renormalizes; standard formula yields sum ~1.

For line search with LP direction, we can avoid full sort by convex combo no projection needed if g simplex. Good.

def interpolate_to_len(a, new_n):
   old=len(a); x=np.linspace(0,1,old); y=np.linspace(0,1,new_n); v=np.interp(y,x,np.maximum(a,0)); v=np.maximum(v,0); s=v.sum(); if tiny return uniform; return v/s
```

def get_cvx_solver():
  global
  if _LP_SOLVER: return
  if cp is None: return 'ECOS'
  for s in ['CLARABEL','PDLP','ECOS','SCS','GLPK','CBC']:
    try: X=cp.Variable(10,nonneg=True); prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(X)), [cp.sum(X)==1, X<=0.5]); prob.solve(solver=s,verbose=False); if status optimal: _LP_SOLVER=s; return
  _LP_SOLVER='ECOS'; return
```

def direct_polish(a, time_limit):
   a=norm_sum1(a)
   best_seq=a; best_score=direct_score(a)
   b=fast_conv(a); best_M=b.max()
   t0=time.monotonic(); K=32; it=0
   while time < time_limit:
      # gradient direction
      peaks=np.argsort(b)[::-1][:K]
      w=...; grad=grad_top(a,peaks,w)
      for step in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
         trial=project_simplex(a - step*grad)
         sc=direct_score(trial)
         if sc<best_score: accept; update b break
      # random moves
      for _ in range(5):
         # choose src weighted by grad? dst random low grad? 
         src = ...; dst=...
         frac random; trial=...
         sc=direct_score(trial)
      it+=1
   return best,best_score
```

`fast_conv(a)`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    Y=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(Y*Y,N)[:L]
    # remove tiny imaginary, clip negative?
    b = np.real(b)
    # symmetrize to reduce numerical asymmetry
    b = 0.5*(b + b[::-1])
    # set extremely small negatives to 0
    # b[b<0 & >-1e-15]=0? But if negative due actual? actual nonneg. Use b=np.maximum(b,0.0) may hide small peaks? max unaffected.
    # However if all positive, max okay. If due asymmetry, sym okay.
    return b
```
Symmetrizing with `b[::-1]` may alter if b not exactly symmetric due truncation? Linear conv self symmetric exactly; FFT error symmetric? Good. Use `b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)`? If there is a true peak tiny negative impossible. But if due roundoff a peak at max could be negative? no. Clipping negatives to 0 can only lower max (if max positive). Fine. But if all zeros? no.
Potential issue: For very sparse sequence with large entries, b may have negative due FFT? max okay. Use `b[b<0]=0` maybe cost O(L). fine.

`fast_max(a)` uses `fast_conv(a).max()`.

`norm_sum1`:
```
def norm_sum1(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a[np.minimum? can't modify copy] = a.clip(0,1000?) No, normalized sum1 so no clip needed.
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <=0: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
   return a/s
```
If a contains NaN, replace 0. `np.nan_to_num`.

`project_simplex`: Need handle v size 0? no.

`peak_weights`:
```
def peak_weights(b, peaks, beta):
    vals=b[peaks]
    v=vals - vals.max()
    if beta <=0: w=np.ones(len(peaks))/len(peaks)
    else:
       # avoid underflow: v/beta scale
       e=np.exp(beta*v)
       s=e.sum(); if s<=0: uniform
       w=e/s
    return w
```
Set beta parameter maybe 1/M*0.05? Since differences small. Instead pass temperature: `beta = 50.0 / max(M,1e-15)`? If M=1e-4, beta=500k. Differences 1e-6 -> exp(0.5). Good. Use `beta = 30.0 / max(M,1e-12)` in gradient. For LP not needed.

`grad_top`: weights sum1. Ensure `weights` same length. If weights sum not 1? normalize. Use factor2.

```
def grad_top(a, peaks, weights, factor=2.0):
   out=np.zeros(a.size,dtype=np.float64)
   for k,w in zip(peaks,weights):
      if w==0: continue
      k=int(k)
      n=a.size
      if k < n:
         out[:k+1] += w * a[k::-1]
      else:
         s=k-n+1
         out[s:n] += w * a[k:s-1:-1]
   return factor*out
```
Check for k<0? peaks 0..2n-2. Good.

Weights for gradient maybe not influence sum direction? Since gradient of max uses weights sum1; row contributions nonnegative. Good.

`select_top_peaks(b,K)`:
```
K=min(K,b.size)
idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:] maybe faster than argsort full? b size ~2n; argsort okay. For return sorted by value descending: idx=np.argsort(b)[::-1][:K].
```
Could include peaks if b close to M. Simpler topK.

`active_lp_descent` implementation more detailed:
```
def active_lp_descent(a_in, time_limit, K_start=128, K_max=None, verbose=True):
   a=norm_sum1(a_in); n=a.size; t0=time.monotonic()
   b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max(); best=a.copy(); best_M=M
   K=K_start; maxK=K_max or min(2048, max(128, 8000000//n?))
   no_improve=0; K_increase_streak=0
   it=0
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
      it+=1
      remaining=time_limit - (time.monotonic()-t0)
      if remaining < 5: break
      b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max()
      if M < best_M - 1e-15: best=a.copy(); best_M=M; no_improve=0? already
      peaks=select_peaks(b,K)
      # if all constraints nearly same and LP failed? 
      g,tau = get_lp_direction(a,b,M,peaks, timeout=min(remaining*0.2,8.0))
      if g is None:
         # fallback: try gradient point if time
         g = None
      if g is not None:
         trial_a,M_trial = line_search_lp(a,g,M)
         if M_trial < M - 1e-15:
             a=trial_a; M=M_trial; no_improve=0
             if M<best_M: best=a.copy(); best_M=M
         else:
             no_improve+=1
      else:
         no_improve += 5
      if no_improve >= 8:
         no_improve=0
         if K < K_max:
             K = min(K_max, K*2)
             continue? (recompute)
         else:
             # perturb
             noise=0.01/r?; a=perturb(best); ...
      if K < K_max and it % 25 ==0 and no_improve==0? maybe gradually increase K.
      if verbose and it%10==0: print
   return best
```
Need avoid `continue` losing time? okay.
Line search function:
```
def line_search_lp(a,g,M):
   b_devs = [1.0,0.75,0.5,0.35,0.25,0.15,0.08,0.04]
   best_a=a; best_M=M
   for alpha in b_devs:
       z=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*g
       # nonneg and sum
       if z.min() < -1e-12: continue
       z=np.maximum(z,0.0); s=z.sum(); if s<=0: continue; z/=s
       m=fast_max(z)
       if m < best_M - 1e-15:
           best_M=m; best_a=z
   return best_a,best_M
```
Could include α=1 always. Since a,g sum1, z sum1 except numerical.

LP fallback if `g is None`: We can compute `g=project_simplex(a - step*grad)` one of steps and line search? But active_lp_descent can simply call `projected_top_step`? Simpler: if LP None, set a = projected_top_one_iter best and continue. But then active_lp logic complex. Maybe structure has separate phases; not rely on LP.

Now, think about the actual objective and LP dual. We might be able to solve LP more efficiently using `scipy.optimize.linprog` with dense C matrix but large constant. Let's code robustly.

```
def get_lp_direction(a,b,M,peaks,timeout=5.0):
    n=len(a); K=len(peaks)
    if K==0: return None,None
    rhs=0.5*(M + b[peaks])
    # If peaks not sorted, no issue.
    use_dense = K*(n+1) <= 4_000_000
    c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
    eq=np.ones((1,n+1), dtype=np.float64); eq[0,-1]=0.0; beq=np.array([1.0])
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)] + [(None,None)]
    res=None
    if opt is not None:
       try:
          if use_dense:
             A=np.zeros((K,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
             for r,k in enumerate(peaks):
                if k<n:
                   A[r,:k+1]=a[k::-1]
                else:
                   s=k-n+1; A[r,s:n]=a[k:s-1:-1]
             A[:,-1]=-1.0
             A_ub=A
          else:
             A=sp.lil_matrix((K,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
             for r,k in enumerate(peaks): ...
             A[:,-1]=-1.0
             A_ub=sp.csr_matrix(A)
          # try highs
          try:
             res=opt.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=rhs, A_eq=eq, b_eq=beq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'time_limit':timeout})
          except Exception:
             res=opt.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=rhs, A_eq=eq, b_eq=beq, bounds=bounds, method='interior-point', options={'maxiter':max(1000,int(timeout*100))})
          if res.status==0:
             g=np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64); tau=float(res.x[-1])
             g=np.maximum(g,0); s=g.sum(); if s>0: return g/s,tau
       except Exception:
          pass
    # cvx fallback
    if cp is not None:
       try:
          solver=get_cvx_solver()
          g_var=cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True); t=cp.Variable()

def build_lp_matrices(a, b, M, peaks):
    n=len(a); K=len(peaks); rhs=0.5*(M + b[peaks])
    if K*(n+1) <= 4_000_000:
       A=np.zeros((K,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
       for r,k in enumerate(peaks):
          k=int(k)
          if k < n:
             A[r, :k+1] = a[k::-1]
          else:
             s=k-n+1
             A[r, s:n] = a[k:s-1:-1]
       A[:,-1] = -1.0
       return A, rhs
    else:
       A=sp.lil_matrix((K,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
       for r,k in enumerate(peaks):
          ...
       A[:,-1] = -1.0
       return sp.csr_matrix(A), rhs
```

   n=len(a); a=norm_sum1(a)

       if time... break

Let's write `project_simplex` carefully for performance. For v already sum~1? If we project x = a - step*grad, sum may <1; theta positive. Standard formula:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv-1.0))[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    r=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[r]-1.0)/(r+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return w/s
```
This returns sum1. For n large, `np.arange` each call. We can precompute? Top-level function can't close but can use global cache? Simpler: compute `rank=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)` each call; cost small. Could optimize with `np.float64(np.arange(...))`. We'll keep.

Now, could use `scipy.special.softmax`? no need.

def perturb_sequence(a, sigma=0.01, seed):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   noise = rng.normal(0, sigma, a.size) * a  # relative
   z=a+noise; z=np.maximum(z,1e-15); z/=z.sum(); return z
```

Let's design `solve_lp_with_matrices(A0, rhs, n, tau_lb, timeout)`:
```
def solve_lp(A0, rhs, n, tau_lb, timeout):
    c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1
    # A_ub: if dense hstack; if sparse hstack
    if isinstance(A0, np.ndarray):
       A_ub=np.hstack((A0, -np.ones((A0.shape[0],1), dtype=np.float64)))
    else:
       A_ub=sp.hstack((A0, sp.csr_matrix(-np.ones((A0.shape[0],1), dtype=np.float64))))
    A_eq=np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,-1]=0
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)] + [(tau_lb, None)]
    # try highs
    try:
      res=opt.linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=rhs,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=[1.0],bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={... 'time_limit':timeout})
      if res.status==0: return ...
    except:
      try interior...
    # cvx fallback with same A0,rhs,tau_lb,timeout
```

def top_gradient_descent(a_in, time_limit, K_start=96, K_max=512, verbose=True):
   a=norm_sum1(a_in); b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max(); best=a.copy(); bestM=M; n=len(a); t0=time.monotonic(); K=K_start; no=0; it=0; temp_factor=0.1; rng=np.random.default_rng(432)
   steps=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0,10.0,20.0]
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
      it+=1
      rem=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)
      if rem<2: break
      b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max()
      if M < bestM - 1e-15: best=a.copy(); bestM=M
      peaks=top_peaks(b,K)
      vals=b[peaks]; temp=max(M*temp_factor, 1e-300); w=np.exp((vals-vals.max())/temp); s=w.sum(); if s<=0: uniform else w/=s
      grad=grad_top(a,peaks,w)
      # line search
      best_trial_a=a; best_trial_M=M
      for step in steps:
         z=project_simplex(a - step*grad)
         m=fast_max(z)
         if m < best_trial_M - 1e-15: best_trial_M=m; best_trial_a=z
      if best_trial_M < M - 1e-14:
          a=best_trial_a; M=best_trial_M; no=0
          if M<bestM: best=a.copy(); bestM=M
          if verbose and it%50==0 print
      else:
          no+=1
          if no==10: temp_factor*=0.6
          if no==25:
             if K<K_max: K*=2
          if no>=60:
             # perturb best
             noise=rng.normal(0,0.02, n)*best
             z=best+noise; z=np.maximum(z,1e-12); z/=z.sum()
             a=z; b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max(); no=0; temp_factor=max(0.05, temp_factor*0.5)? maybe
      it...
   return best
```

```
def get_cvx_solver():
  global _CVX_SOLVER
  if _CVX_SOLVER is not None: return _CVX_SOLVER
  candidates=[cp.CLARABEL, cp.PDLP, cp.ECOS, cp.SCS, cp.GLPK, cp.CBC]
  for s in candidates:
    try:
      x=cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True)
      prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x)==1, x<=0.5])
      prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
      if prob.status in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate'):
         _CVX_SOLVER=s; return s
    except Exception: continue
  _CVX_SOLVER=cp.ECOS; return _CVX_SOLVER
```

    a=norm_sum1(a_in); n=a.size; t0=time.monotonic(); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=fast_max(a); best_shift=0
    if full:
       cands=list(range(n))
    else:
       step=max(1,n//300)
       cands=list(range(0,n,step))
       rng=np.random.default_rng(1234+n % 10000)
       if n_samples >0:
          cands.extend(int(x) for x in rng.integers(0,n,size=min(n_samples,n))) # generator expression okay? Not lambda. But list extend with generator.
       cands.append(0)
       cands=list(set(cands)) # order random but okay
    for s in cands:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       ar=np.roll(a, -int(s))
       m=fast_max(ar)
       if m < best_M - 1e-15:
           best_M=m; best_shift=int(s); best_a=ar.copy()
    if not full and n > 1:
       # local full around best_shift
       width = min(local_window, max(10, n//50))
       for ds in range(-width, width+1):
           s=(best_shift+ds)%n
           if s==best_shift: continue
           ar=np.roll(a,-s); m=fast_max(ar); update
          if time exceeded break
    # refine ±3
    for ds in range(-3,4):...
    return best_a,best_M,best_shift
```

`interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(new_n)/max(1,new_n)
    old_n=a.size
    if old_n==new_n:
       return norm_sum1(a)
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    v=np.interp(new_x, old_x, np.maximum(a,0.0))
    v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
    s=v.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    return v/s
```

`smooth_candidate`:
```
def smooth_candidate(n, kind=0):
   if n<=0: return []
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   if kind==0:
       a=1.0 + 0.7*np.cos(np.pi*x)
   elif kind==1:
       a=1.0 + 0.7*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x)
       # cos full period high at 0,1 middle? positive min0.3
   elif kind==2:
       a=np.sin(np.pi*x)**2 + 0.3
   else:
       a=np.ones(n)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-8); s=a.sum(); return a/s
```

def active_lp_descent(a_in, time_limit, K_start=128, K_max=None, verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a_in); n=a.size; t0=time.monotonic()
    if K_max is None: K_max=max_lp_K(n)
    K=K_start
    best=a.copy(); b=fast_conv(a); best_M=b.max(); M=best_M
    no=0; fail=0; tau_frac=0.1
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it+=1
       rem=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)
       if rem < 4 or M <= 1e-15: break
       b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max()
       if M < best_M - 1e-15: best=a.copy(); best_M=M
       peaks=top_peaks(b,K)
       timeout=min(6.0, max(1.0, rem*0.2))
       tau_lb=-tau_frac*M
       g,tau=get_lp_direction(a,b,M,peaks,timeout,tau_lb)
       if g is None:
          fail+=1
          if fail>4: break
          continue
       fail=0
       trial_a, trial_M = line_search_lp(a,g,M)
       if trial_M < M - 1e-15:
           a=trial_a; M=trial_M; no=0
           if M < best_M - 1e-15: best=a.copy(); best_M=M
           if verbose and it%50==0: print...
       else:
           no+=1
           if no >= 8:
               if K < K_max:
                   K = min(K_max, K*2)
                   no=0
                   continue
               if tau_frac < 0.5:
                   tau_frac = min(1.0, tau_frac*2.0)
                   no=0
                   continue
               # perturb if still stuck
               if no > 60: break
               if no == 40:
                   rng... perturb from best
       if verbose and it%200==0: print
    return best
```

`peak_weights(b, peaks, temp_factor)`:
```
def peak_weights(b, peaks, temp_factor=0.1):
    vals=b[peaks]
    if vals.size==0: return np.zeros(0)
    vmax=vals.max(); temp=max(vmax*temp_factor, 1e-300)
    x=(vals-vmax)/temp
    w=np.exp(x)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       w=np.ones(vals.size)/vals.size
    else:
       w=w/s
    return w
```
If temp_factor=0, use uniform? Add if temp_factor <=0. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function height sequence minimizing

    eval(a) = 2*n*max(np.convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

The optimizer combines:
- active-set linearized Frank-Wolfe/LP local search
- top-peak projected gradient descents
- cyclic-shift scouting
- length extrapolation / rescanning at larger n
- final direct-evaluator polishing
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as sopt
except Exception:
    sopt = None

try:
    import scipy.sparse as sp
except Exception:
    sp = None

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

_CVX_SOLVER = None


def get_cvx_solver():
    """Pick a CVXPY LP/QP solver if one is available."""
    global _CVX_SOLVER
    if _CVX_SOLVER is not None:
        return _CVX_SOLVER
    if cp is None:
        return "ECOS"
    for name in ["CLARABEL", "PDLP", "ECOS", "SCS", "GLPK", "CBC"]:
        s = getattr(cp, name, None)
        if s is None:
            continue
        try:
            x = cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True)
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 0.5])
            prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                _CVX_SOLVER = s
                return _CVX_SOLVER
        except Exception:
            pass
    _CVX_SOLVER = "ECOS"
    return _CVX_SOLVER


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution via FFT, symmetrized and clipped to nonnegative."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    Y = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(Y * Y, N)[:L]
    b = np.real(b)
    b = 0.5 * (b + b[::-1])
    b[b < 0.0] = 0.0
    return b


def fast_max(a):
    return float(fast_conv(a).max())


def internal_score(a):
    """Score assuming the sequence is scaled to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * fast_max(a))


def norm_sum1(a):
    """Project to nonnegative simplex with numerical safeguards."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def project_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto the probability simplex."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) > (cssv - 1.0))[0]
    if len(rho) == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    r = rho[-1]
    theta = (cssv[r] - 1.0) / (r + 1.0)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def top_peaks(b, K):
    """Indices of the K largest convolution coefficients."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    K = int(min(K, b.size))
    if K == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if K == b.size:
        return np.argsort(b)[::-1].astype(int)
    idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    idx = idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[::-1]]
    return idx.astype(int)


def peak_weights(b, peaks, temp_factor=0.1):
    """Soft weights over selected peak indices."""
    valleys = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)[peaks]
    m = valleys.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if temp_factor <= 0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    vmax = float(valleys.max())
    temp = max(1e-300, vmax * float(temp_factor))
    x = (valleys - vmax) / temp
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def grad_top(a, peaks, weights, factor=2.0):
    """
    Gradient of a convex combination of selected convolution coefficients.

    For c_k = (a*a)_k, d c_k / d a_l = 2 a_{k-l} when valid.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, w in zip(np.asarray(peaks, dtype=int), np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64)):
        if w == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        if k < n:
            out[: k + 1] += w * a[k::-1]
        else:
            s = k - n + 1
            out[s:n] += w * a[k : s - 1 : -1]
    return float(factor) * out


def direct_score(a):
    """Call the external evaluator on a list of Python floats."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = arr.sum()
        if s < 0.01:
            return float("inf")
        seq = [float(x) for x in arr]
        sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            return float(sc)
        return float("inf")
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, a, label, t_global):
    """Direct-evaluate candidate and update best if it is better."""
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    sc = direct_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and (not np.isfinite(best_score) or sc < best_score - 1e-13):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_global
        print("  *** NEW DIRECT BEST [" + str(label) + "]: " + repr(sc) +
              " (n=" + str(a.size) + ", t=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s)")
        return a, sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def update_work(work_seq, work_score, a):
    """Keep the best internally scoring candidate for continued optimization."""
    if a is None:
        return work_seq, work_score
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return work_seq, work_score
    sc = internal_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and (not np.isfinite(work_score) or sc < work_score - 1e-12):
        return norm_sum1(a), sc
    return work_seq, work_score


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    """L1-normalized linear resampling to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(new_n)), dtype=np.float64)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == new_n:
        return norm_sum1(a)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    v = np.interp(new_x, old_x, np.maximum(a, 0.0))
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = v.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return v / s


def smooth_candidate(n, kind=0):
    """Fallback analytic non-negative candidates over [0,1]."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if kind == 0:
        a = 1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)   # symmetric U-shape
    elif kind == 1:
        a = np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2 + 0.35         # center-heavy
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a / s


def max_lp_K(n):
    if n <= 4000:
        return 1024
    if n <= 8000:
        return 768
    if n <= 14000:
        return 512
    return 256


def build_lp_A0(a, peaks):
    """
    Matrix A0 such that (A0 @ g)_r = (a * g)_{peaks[r]}.
    Uses dense when feasible, sparse otherwise.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    peaks = np.asarray(peaks, dtype=int)
    n = a.size
    K = peaks.size
    if K == 0:
        return np.zeros((0, n), dtype=np.float64)

    use_sparse = (sp is not None) and (K * (n + 1) > 6_000_000)

    if use_sparse:
        A = sp.lil_matrix((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for r, k in enumerate(peaks):
            k = int(k)
            if k < n:
                cols = np.arange(0, k + 1, dtype=int)
                A[r, cols] = a[k::-1]
            else:
                s = k - n + 1
                cols = np.arange(s, n, dtype=int)
                A[r, cols] = a[k : s - 1 : -1]
        return sp.csr_matrix(A)
    else:
        A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for r, k in enumerate(peaks):
            k = int(k)
            if k < n:
                A[r, : k + 1] = a[k::-1]
            else:
                s = k - n + 1
                A[r, s:n] = a[k : s - 1 : -1]
        return A


def solve_lp_with_matrices(A0, rhs, n, tau_lb, timeout=5.0):
    """
    Solve:
      min tau
      s.t. A0 @ g - tau <= rhs
           sum(g)=1, g>=0, tau>=tau_lb
    Returns (g, tau) or (None, None).
    """
    if n == 0 or A0.shape[0] == 0:
        return None, None

    use_scipy = sopt is not None
    if use_scipy:
        try:
            c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            c_obj[-1] = 1.0

            if isinstance(A0, np.ndarray):
                tau_col = -np.ones((A0.shape[0], 1), dtype=np.float64)
                A_ub = np.hstack((A0, tau_col))
            else:
                tau_col = sp.csr_matrix(-np.ones((A0.shape[0], 1), dtype=np.float64))
                A_ub = sp.hstack((A0, tau_col))

            A_eq = np.ones((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
            A_eq[0, -1] = 0.0
            b_eq = np.array([1.0])
            bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n + [(float(tau_lb), None)]

            res = None
            try:
                res = sopt.linprog(
                    c_obj,
                    A_ub=A_ub,
                    b_ub=np.asarray(rhs, dtype=np.float64),
                    A_eq=A_eq,
                    b_eq=b_eq,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="highs",
                    options={
                        "presolve": True,
                        "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
                        "primal_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
                        "gap_rel": 1e-10,
                        "time_limit": max(1.0, float(timeout)),
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                try:
                    res = sopt.linprog(
                        c_obj,
                        A_ub=A_ub,
                        b_ub=np.asarray(rhs, dtype=np.float64),
                        A_eq=A_eq,
                        b_eq=b_eq,
                        bounds=bounds,
                        method="highs",
                        options={"presolve": True},
                    )
                except Exception:
                    res = sopt.linprog(
                        c_obj,
                        A_ub=A_ub,
                        b_ub=np.asarray(rhs, dtype=np.float64),
                        A_eq=A_eq,
                        b_eq=b_eq,
                        bounds=bounds,
                        method="interior-point",
                        options={"maxiter": max(1000, int(timeout * 50))},
                    )

            if res is not None and res.status == 0:
                g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
                tau = float(res.x[-1])
                g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
                s = g.sum()
                if s > 1e-300:
                    return g / s, tau
        except Exception:
            pass

    if cp is not None:
        try:
            solver = get_cvx_solver()
            g_var = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
            t_var = cp.Variable()
            constraints = [
                cp.sum(g_var) == 1,
                t_var >= float(tau_lb),
                A0 @ g_var - t_var <= np.asarray(rhs, dtype=np.float64),
            ]
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t_var), constraints)
            try:
                prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False, solver_opts={"time_limit": int(max(1, timeout))})
            except Exception:
                prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                g = np.asarray(g_var.value, dtype=np.float64)
                tau = float(t_var.value)
                g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
                s = g.sum()
                if s > 1e-300:
                    return g / s, tau
        except Exception:
            pass

    return None, None


def get_lp_direction(a, b, M, peaks, timeout=5.0, tau_lb=0.0):
    """LP oracle for the linearized active-peak problem."""
    if a is None or M is None or M <= 0.0 or len(peaks) == 0:
        return None, None
    rhs = 0.5 * (M + np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)[peaks])
    try:
        A0 = build_lp_A0(a, peaks)
        return solve_lp_with_matrices(A0, rhs, a.size, tau_lb, timeout)
    except Exception:
        return None, None


def line_search_lp(a, g, M):
    """Safe convex-combination line search for a simplex direction g."""
    if a is None or g is None:
        return a, M
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.shape != g.shape:
        return a, M
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = float(M)
    alphas = [1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.45, 0.3, 0.2, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03]
    for alpha in alphas:
        z = a + float(alpha) * (g - a)
        if z.min() < -1e-12:
            continue
        z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
        s = z.sum()
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        z = z / s
        m = fast_max(z)
        if m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best_a = z
    return best_a, best_m


def active_lp_descent(a_in, time_limit, K_start=128, K_max=None, verbose=True):
    """
    Active-set linearized descent:
    solve a small LP to obtain a simplex direction that reduces the
    linearized active convolution peaks, then exact line search in time-domain.
    """
    a = norm_sum1(a_in)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <= 2.0:
        return a

    if K_max is None:
        K_max = max_lp_K(n)
    K = int(min(K_start, max(1, K_max)))
    K_max = int(max(1, K_max))

    b = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    no_improve = 0
    fail_count = 0
    tau_frac = 0.10
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 4.0 or M <= 1e-15:
            break

        b = fast_conv(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        peaks = top_peaks(b, K)
        timeout = max(1.0, min(6.0, 0.2 * rem))
        tau_lb = -float(tau_frac) * M

        g, tau = get_lp_direction(a, b, M, peaks, timeout=timeout, tau_lb=tau_lb)
        if g is None:
            fail_count += 1
            if fail_count > 4:
                break
            continue
        fail_count = 0

        trial_a, trial_M = line_search_lp(a, g, M)
        if trial_M < M - 1e-15:
            a = trial_a
            M = trial_M
            no_improve = 0
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()
            if verbose and (it % 50 == 0):
                print("    ActiveLP it=" + str(it) + " tau=" + repr(float(tau)) +
                      " score=" + repr(2.0 * n * M) +
                      " K=" + str(K))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 8:
                if K < K_max:
                    K = int(min(K_max, K * 2))
                    no_improve = 0
                    if verbose:
                        print("    ActiveLP increasing K to " + str(K))
                    continue
                if tau_frac < 0.5:
                    tau_frac = min(1.0, tau_frac * 2.0)
                    no_improve = 0
                    continue
            if no_improve >= 40:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(1000 + it)
                noise = 0.02 * rng.normal(size=n) * best_a
                z = best_a + noise
                z = np.maximum(z, 1e-12)
                s = z.sum()
                if s > 1e-300:
                    a = z / s
                    b = fast_conv(a)
                    M = float(b.max())
                no_improve = 0
            if no_improve >= 80:
                break

        if verbose and (it % 250 == 0):
            print("    ActiveLP it=" + str(it) + " score=" + repr(2.0 * n * M))

    return best_a


def top_gradient_descent(a_in, time_limit, K_start=96, K_max=512, verbose=True):
    """
    Projected gradient steps using soft weights over top convolution peaks.
    Robust fallback / long-length optimizer.
    """
    a = norm_sum1(a_in)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 2.0:
        return a
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    b = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    temp_factor = 0.10
    K = int(min(K_start, max(1, K_max)))
    K_max = int(max(1, K_max))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(555)

    steps = [0.001, 0.003, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0]

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 3.0 or M <= 1e-15:
            break

        b = fast_conv(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        peaks = top_peaks(b, K)
        w = peak_weights(b, peaks, temp_factor)
        grad = grad_top(a, peaks, w)
        if not np.any(grad > 0):
            break

        best_tr_a = a.copy()
        best_tr_M = M
        for st in steps:
            z = project_simplex(a - float(st) * grad)
            m = fast_max(z)
            if m < best_tr_M - 1e-15:
                best_tr_M = m
                best_tr_a = z

        if best_tr_M < M - 1e-14:
            a = best_tr_a
            M = best_tr_M
            no_improve = 0
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()
            if verbose and (it % 100 == 0):
                print("    TopGrad it=" + str(it) + " score=" + repr(2.0 * n * M) +
                      " K=" + str(K) + " temp=" + repr(temp_factor))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 12:
                temp_factor *= 0.6
            if no_improve == 30:
                if K < K_max:
                    K = int(min(K_max, K * 2))
            if no_improve == 70:
                noise = 0.015 * rng.normal(size=n) * best_a
                z = best_a + noise
                z = np.maximum(z, 1e-12)
                s = z.sum()
                if s > 1e-300:
                    a = z / s
                    b = fast_conv(a)
                    M = float(b.max())
                temp_factor = max(0.03, temp_factor * 0.5)
                no_improve = 0
            if no_improve >= 150:
                break

    return best_a


def cyclic_shift_best(a_in, full=False, time_limit=60.0, n_samples=420, local_window=90):
    """Find a good cyclic shift by FFT scoring (full if possible/requested)."""
    a = norm_sum1(a_in)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a.copy(), fast_max(a), 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return a.copy(), fast_max(a), 0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = fast_max(a)
    best_shift = 0

    if full:
        cands = list(range(n))
    else:
        cands = []
        step = max(1, n // 300)
        cands.extend(range(0, n, step))
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240407 + (n % 100000))
        m = int(min(n_samples, n))
        if m > 0:
            cands.extend(rng.integers(0, n, size=m).tolist())
        cands.append(0)
        cands = list(np.unique(np.asarray(cands, dtype=int)))

    deadline = t0 + float(time_limit)
    for s in cands:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        s = int(s) % n
        ar = np.roll(a, -s)
        m = fast_max(ar)
        if m < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = m
            best_shift = s
            best_a = ar.copy()

    if not full and n > 1:
        width = int(min(max(10, local_window), max(1, n // 40)))
        for ds in range(-width, width + 1):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            s = (best_shift + ds) % n
            if s == best_shift:
                continue
            ar = np.roll(a, -s)
            m = fast_max(ar)
            if m < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = m
                best_shift = s
                best_a = ar.copy()

        for ds in range(-3, 4):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            s = (best_shift + ds) % n
            if s == best_shift:
                continue
            ar = np.roll(a, -s)
            m = fast_max(ar)
            if m < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = m
                best_shift = s
                best_a = ar.copy()

    return norm_sum1(best_a), float(best_M), int(best_shift)


def direct_polish(a_in, time_limit):
    """
    Final local polish using the true evaluator as the objective.
    Uses internal FFT only to preselect promising moves.
    """
    a = norm_sum1(a_in)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 5.0:
        return a, direct_score(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(time_limit)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = direct_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")

    max_iters = 160 if n <= 8000 else 70
    steps = [0.001, 0.003, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0]
    no_improve = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(98765)

    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        b = fast_conv(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        peaks = top_peaks(b, 32)
        w = peak_weights(b, peaks, 0.1)
        grad = grad_top(a, peaks, w)
        accepted = False

        # Best gradient step by internal metric, then direct verification.
        ms = []
        zs = []
        for st in steps:
            z = project_simplex(a - float(st) * grad)
            ms.append(fast_max(z))
            zs.append(z)
        order = np.argsort(np.asarray(ms, dtype=np.float64))
        z = zs[int(order[0])]
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        sc = direct_score(z)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            a = z
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = sc
            accepted = True
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        # Two randomized coordinate moves guided by gradient.
        if len(peaks) > 0:
            for _ in range(2):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                gpos = np.argsort(grad)[::-1][:20]
                gneg = np.argsort(grad)[:20]
                if len(gpos) == 0 or len(gneg) == 0:
                    break
                src = int(gpos[rng.integers(0, len(gpos))])
                dst = int(gneg[rng.integers(0, len(gneg))])
                if dst == src:
                    continue
                frac = float(rng.choice([0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1]))
                amt = a[src] * frac
                if amt <= 0:
                    continue
                z = a.copy()
                z[src] -= amt
                z[dst] += amt
                z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
                s = z.sum()
                if s <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                z = z / s
                im = fast_max(z)
                if im >= M:
                    continue
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                sc2 = direct_score(z)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                    a = z
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_score = sc2
                    accepted = True
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 18:
                noise = 0.004 * rng.normal(size=n) * best_a
                z = best_a + noise
                z = np.maximum(z, 1e-12)
                s = z.sum()
                if s > 1e-300:
                    a = z / s
                no_improve = 0

        if it % 20 == 0 and np.isfinite(best_score):
            print("    DirectPolish it=" + str(it) + " score=" + repr(best_score))

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Main search driver.
    Returns the best non-negative list found within the time budget.
    """
    t_global0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t_global0 + min(total_budget, 1800.0) - 30.0

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    work_seq = None
    work_score = float("inf")

    try:
        # Load parent construction if available.
        init_seq = None
        try:
            parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
            if parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size > 0:
                p = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
                if p.sum() >= 0.01:
                    init_seq = norm_sum1(p)
        except Exception:
            init_seq = None

        if init_seq is None:
            init_seq = norm_sum1(smooth_candidate(6700, kind=0))

        best_seq = init_seq.copy()
        best_score = direct_score(best_seq)
        work_seq = init_seq.copy()
        work_score = internal_score(work_seq)
        print("Initial: n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + ", direct=" + repr(best_score) +
              ", internal=" + repr(work_score))

        n0 = int(best_seq.size)
        max_len = 18000
        if n0 > max_len:
            max_len = n0

        def remaining():
            return max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())

        # Stage A: optimize the original length.
        rem = remaining()
        if rem > 90 and n0 <= 12000:
            t_lp = min(60 + 0.35 * rem, 700.0)
            if t_lp > 45:
                print("\n=== Stage A1: active LP at n=" + str(n0) + " ===")
                out = active_lp_descent(work_seq, t_lp, K_start=128, K_max=max_lp_K(n0), verbose=True)
                work_seq, work_score = update_work(work_seq, work_score, out)
                best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, out, "activeLP-n0", t_global0)

        rem = remaining()
        if rem > 80:
            print("\n=== Stage A2: cyclic shift n0 ===")
            full = (n0 <= 8000 and rem > 120)
            a_sh, m_sh, _ = cyclic_shift_best(work_seq, full=full, time_limit=min(60.0, 0.12 * rem),
                                              n_samples=700, local_window=120)
            work_seq, work_score = update_work(work_seq, work_score, a_sh)
            best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, a_sh, "shift-n0", t_global0)

        rem = remaining()
        if rem > 60:
            t_g = min(60 + 0.08 * rem, 180.0)
            if t_g > 30:
                print("\n=== Stage A3: top-gradient n0 ===")
                out = top_gradient_descent(work_seq, t_g, K_start=96, K_max=max_lp_K(n0), verbose=True)
                work_seq, work_score = update_work(work_seq, work_score, out)
                best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, out, "grad-n0", t_global0)

        # Stage B: length extrapolation and quick direct validation.
        print("\n=== Stage B: length sweep ===")
        base_len = int(work_seq.size)
        cand_lens = set([base_len])
        for ratio in [1.20, 1.50, 2.00, 2.60, 3.40]:
            nn = int(round(base_len * ratio))
            if nn > base_len:
                cand_lens.add(min(nn, max_len))
        if max_len > base_len:
            cand_lens.add(max_len)

        sweep = []
        for nn in sorted(cand_lens):
            if nn < 100 or nn > max_len:
                continue
            if nn == base_len:
                a_ext = work_seq.copy()
            else:
                a_ext = interpolate_sequence(work_seq, nn)
            sc_int = internal_score(a_ext)
            if np.isfinite(sc_int):
                sweep.append((sc_int, nn, a_ext))

        # Extra analytic shapes at/around the maximum useful length.
        if max_len > base_len:
            for kind in [0, 1, 2]:
                a_alt = smooth_candidate(max_len, kind=kind)
                sc_int = internal_score(a_alt)
                if np.isfinite(sc_int):
                    sweep.append((sc_int, max_len, a_alt))

        if sweep:
            scores = np.array([float(x[0]) for x in sweep], dtype=np.float64)
            order = np.argsort(scores)
            verified = 0
            for pos in order:
                if verified >= 3 and not (sweep[int(pos)][1] == base_len):
                    break
                sc_int, nn, a_ext = sweep[int(pos)]
                # Validate selected candidates with the true evaluator.
                best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, a_ext, "sweep-n" + str(nn), t_global0)
                work_seq, work_score = update_work(work_seq, work_score, a_ext)
                verified += 1
                if verified >= 4:
                    break

        # Stage C: optimize the currently working sequence (possibly longer).
        rem = remaining()
        if rem > 150:
            nf = int(work_seq.size)
            print("\n=== Stage C: optimize working n=" + str(nf) + " ===")
            if nf <= 12000:
                t_lp = min(0.40 * rem, 350.0)
                if t_lp > 60:
                    out = active_lp_descent(work_seq, t_lp, K_start=128, K_max=max_lp_K(nf), verbose=True)
                    work_seq, work_score = update_work(work_seq, work_score, out)
                    best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, out, "activeLP-work", t_global0)

                rem = remaining()
                t_g = min(0.30 * rem, 250.0)
                if t_g > 45:
                    out = top_gradient_descent(work_seq, t_g, K_start=96, K_max=max_lp_K(nf), verbose=True)
                    work_seq, work_score = update_work(work_seq, work_score, out)
                    best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, out, "grad-work", t_global0)
            else:
                t_g = min(0.70 * rem, 900.0)
                if t_g > 45:
                    out = top_gradient_descent(work_seq, t_g, K_start=128, K_max=384, verbose=True)
                    work_seq, work_score = update_work(work_seq, work_score, out)
                    best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, out, "grad-long", t_global0)

                rem = remaining()
                if rem > 80:
                    a_sh, m_sh, _ = cyclic_shift_best(work_seq, full=False,
                                                      time_limit=min(60.0, 0.2 * rem),
                                                      n_samples=700, local_window=120)
                    work_seq, work_score = update_work(work_seq, work_score, a_sh)
                    best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, a_sh, "shift-long", t_global0)

        # Direct-validate the best working candidate before polishing.
        best_seq, best_score = maybe_update(best_seq, best_score, work_seq, "work-final", t_global0)

        # Stage D: true-evaluator polishing.
        rem = remaining()
        if rem > 60:
            print("\n=== Stage D: direct polish ===")
            t_polish = max(25.0, min(180.0, rem - 15.0))
            polished, polished_score = direct_polish(best_seq, t_polish)
            if polished is not None and np.isfinite(polished_score):
                if not np.isfinite(best_score) or polished_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_seq = polished
                    best_score = polished_score
                else:
                    # Even if the reported best did not move, keep sequence valid.
                    pass

    except Exception:
        print("Recovery from exception; returning current best/default.")

    # Final safety fallback.
    if best_seq is None or len(best_seq) == 0:
        if work_seq is not None and len(work_seq) > 0:
            best_seq = work_seq
        else:
            best_seq = norm_sum1(smooth_candidate(6700, kind=0))

    best_seq = norm_sum1(best_seq)
    best_seq = np.nan_to_num(best_seq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)

    # One last direct check (best effort).
    try:
        sc = direct_score(best_seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_score = sc
    except Exception:
        pass

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        # If the evaluator could not be reached for some reason, still return a valid sequence.
        pass

    out = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
    try:
        print("\nFINAL: n=" + str(len(out)) + ", score=" + repr(best_score) +
              ", elapsed=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t_global0, 1)) + "s")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out
```
